Runtime RAM objects; values are not resident here in IMG ROM. Bases: 0x408ef72c 0x408ef7a8 0x408ef824 0x408ef8a0 0x408ef91c 0x408ef998 0x408efa14 0x408efa90

=== INTERPRETATION RULE ===
A constant store of 16 and 22 to +0x5C/+0x60 would close 16x22 Block_Cnt geometry. If writers copy values from a caller/config structure instead, trace that source next rather than assigning semantics by expected grid size.
